hi=mins(datetime(2024,1,16,2,0))\n    for tb10 in range(int(lo*10),int(hi*10)+1,5):\n        tb=tb10/10.0\n        r=solve3(xs,ys,tb)\n        if r: prof.append((r[1],tb,r[0]))\n    prof.sort()\n    sse,tb,sol=prof[0]\n    dof=len(xs)-3\n    # 95% CI: SSE within best*(1+ F/dof) approx -> use chi2-ish threshold\n    thr=sse*(1+3.84/dof)\n    ok=[p[1] for p in prof if p[0]<=thr]\n    delay=float(m['response_delay_minutes'])\n    phys=tb-delay\n    res[sid]=(m['subsystem'],tb,phys,min(ok)-delay,max(ok)-delay,sol[2]/sd)\n    print('%-17s %-13s  %s   [%s,%s]  %4d   %s    %+.2f sd/hr'%(\n        sid,m['subsystem'],tm(tb),tm(min(ok)),tm(max(ok)),int(delay),tm(phys),sol[1]*60/sd))\npickle.dump(res,open('/app/analysis/res3.pkl','wb'))\nprint()\nprint('=== SUBSYSTEM PHYSICAL ONSET (inverse-variance-ish: use earliest & median) ===')\nsub={}\nfor sid,(ss,o,p,plo,phi,dr) in res.items(): sub.setdefault(ss,[]).append((p,plo,phi,sid))\norder=[]\nfor ss,lst in sub.items():\n    lst.sort()\n    ps=sorted(x[0] for x in lst)\n    med=(ps[1]+ps[2])/2\n    order.append((ps[0],med,ss,lst))\nfor ps0,med,ss,lst in sorted(order,key=lambda z:z[1]):\n    print('%-13s earliest=%s median=%s  |  %s'%(ss,tm(ps0),tm(med),\n       ', '.join('%s %s[%s-%s]'%(s.split('_',1)[1],tm(p),tm(a),tm(b)) for p,a,b,s in lst)))\nEOF\npython3 -I /app/analysis/fit3.py","description":"Refit changepoints with baseline drift term and CIs"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2375,"cache_read_input_tokens":49287,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2375,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":9,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"93dd3b3d-27db-414a-a7cc-ce40a3c4833d","uuid":"915a146f-f011-46d7-9b37-aa2b848c9383","timestamp":"2026-10-15T15:44:03.784Z"}

tot=0\npairwins={}\nfor b in range(B):\n    sub={}\n    for sid in meta:\n        xs,pred,resid,tb0=fitted[sid]\n        ys=[p+random.choice(resid) for p in pred]\n        r=fit(xs,ys)\n        sub.setdefault(meta[sid]['subsystem'],[]).append(r[1]-float(meta[sid]['response_delay_minutes']))\n    m2={k:st.median(v) for k,v in sub.items()}\n    for k,v in m2.items(): meds[k].append(v)\n    ordr=[k for _,k in sorted((v,k) for k,v in m2.items())]\n    pairwins[tuple(ordr)]=pairwins.get(tuple(ordr),0)+1\n\nprint('Residual bootstrap, B=%d\\n'%B)\nfor ss,v in sorted(meds.items(), key=lambda kv: st.median(kv[1])):\n    v=sorted(v)\n    print('%-13s median=%s  95%%CI=[%s, %s]'%(ss,tm(st.median(v)),tm(v[int(.025*len(v))]),tm(v[int(.975*len(v))-1])))\nprint('\\nBootstrapped full-order frequencies:')\nfor o,c in sorted(pairwins.items(),key=lambda kv:-kv[1]):\n    print('  %5.1f%%  %s'%(100*c/B,' -> '.join(o)))\nEOF\npython3 -I /app/analysis/boot.py","description":"Residual bootstrap on subsystem onset ordering"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2141,"cache_read_input_tokens":69116,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2141,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":7,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"93dd3b3d-27db-414a-a7cc-ce40a3c4833d","uuid":"e9e8ac9c-cdc3-4631-a48c-d2d6af9928bd","timestamp":"2026-10-15T15:50:37.521Z"}

The fit window ends at 02:45, before the first operator action (emergency shutdown at 02:52) perturbs the system.
